<article class="w-grid-item size_1x1 post-59728 post type-post status-publish format-standard has-post-thumbnail hentry category-kultura category-zivotni-stil tag-prica naslovnica-zivotni-stil jezik-hrvatski" data-id="59728">
<div class="w-grid-item-h">
<a aria-label="Koga li si prevario, sine?" class="w-grid-item-anchor" href="https://www.logicno.com/zivotni-stil/kultura/koga-li-si-prevario-sine.html" rel="bookmark"></a>
<div class="w-hwrapper usg_hwrapper_1 align_left valign_top"><div class="w-post-elm post_image usg_post_image_1 stretched"><a aria-label="Koga li si prevario, sine?" href="https://www.logicno.com/zivotni-stil/kultura/koga-li-si-prevario-sine.html"><img alt="Prase svinja bravac gica" class="attachment-us_205_205_crop size-us_205_205_crop wp-post-image" height="205" sizes="(max-width: 205px) 100vw, 205px" src="https://www.logicno.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Prase-svinja-bravac-gica-205x205.jpg" srcset="https://www.logicno.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Prase-svinja-bravac-gica-205x205.jpg 205w, https://www.logicno.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Prase-svinja-bravac-gica-146x146.jpg 146w, https://www.logicno.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Prase-svinja-bravac-gica-99x99.jpg 99w, https://www.logicno.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Prase-svinja-bravac-gica-75x75.jpg 75w, https://www.logicno.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Prase-svinja-bravac-gica-64x64.jpg 64w, https://www.logicno.com/wp-content/uploads/2019/10/Prase-svinja-bravac-gica-62x62.jpg 62w" width="205"/></a></div><div class="w-vwrapper usg_vwrapper_1 align_left valign_top"><div class="w-post-elm post_custom_field usg_post_custom_field_1 with_text_color type_text color_link_inherit"><a href="https://www.logicno.com/zivotni-stil/kultura/koga-li-si-prevario-sine.html">Priča</a></div><h2 class="w-post-elm post_title usg_post_title_1 entry-title color_link_inherit"><a href="https://www.logicno.com/zivotni-stil/kultura/koga-li-si-prevario-sine.html">Koga li si prevario, sine?</a></h2><div class="w-post-elm post_content usg_post_content_1">Nekada i u naizgled najpoštenijim poslovima ima neposlovnosti, prljavštine i prevara, a i u najnepoštenijim ima poštenja. I najokorjelije mafijaške organizacije nekada…</div><div class="w-hwrapper usg_hwrapper_2 align_left valign_top"><div class="w-post-elm post_author usg_post_author_1 color_link_inherit vcard author"><div class="post-author-meta"><a class="post-author-name fn" href="https://www.logicno.com/author/ik001">Ivo Kobaš</a></div></div><time class="w-post-elm post_date usg_post_date_1 with_text_color entry-date published" datetime="2019-10-23T04:56:25+02:00">23.  lis. 2019. 4:56</time></div></div><div class="w-vwrapper usg_vwrapper_2 align_center valign_top"><div class="w-post-elm post_comments usg_post_comments_2 layout_amount color_link_inherit"><i class="far fa-comment"></i><a class="smooth-scroll" href="https://www.logicno.com/zivotni-stil/kultura/koga-li-si-prevario-sine.html#comments">2 <span class="screen-reader-text">komentara</span></a></div></div></div> </div>
